function AttackMultipleTargets(K, ...)
	if not RegisterHit or not RegisterAttack then
		return;
	end;
	pcall(function(...)
		if not K or #K == -597271 - (-597271) then
			return;
		end;
		local o = {};
		for K, d in pairs(K) do
			local V = d:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(-1077406 - (-1014609)), 18889831397736 - 792332)]);
			if V then
				table[u1[U1(W1(-712353 - (-650397)), 21509141824433 - (-484206))]](o, { d, V });
			end;
		end;
		if #o == 714514 - 714514 then
			return;
		end;
		RegisterAttack:FireServer(104605592 % 574756);
		RegisterHit:FireServer(o[967346 + -967345][-903025 - (-903027)], o);
	end);
end;

function isAttackAnim(K, ...)
	local o = string[u1[U1(W1(519125 + -581812), -321053 + 30000113409198)]](K[u1[U1(W1(-23534 + -36128), 26783520185335 - (-791745))]]);
	for K, d in ipairs(ATTACK_KEYWORDS) do
		if string[u1[U1(W1(-839728 + 779930), 9295384107927 - 363183)]](o, d) then
			return true;
		end;
	end;
	if K[u1[U1(W1(284665 + -346671), 7186992506244 - (-75662))]] == Enum[u1[U1(W1(769972 + -832783), 656058 + 16987816926252)]][u1[U1(W1(333797 + -395614), 24833515606707 - (-1038969))]] then
		return true;
	end;
	return false;
end;
function StopPlayerAnimations(...)
	local K = player[u1[U1(W1(760773 - 821674), 26652706365022 - (-987677))]];
	if not K then
		return;
	end;
	local o = K:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(-917429 + 858106), 23182959279264 - (-585099))]);
	if not o then
		return;
	end;
	local d = o:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(-166406 - (-104199)), -942779 + 18137088476088)]);
	if not d then
		return;
	end;
	for K, o in pairs(d:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
		if not isAttackAnim(o) then
			o:Stop(596595 - 596595);
		end;
	end;
end;

function isFlashstep(K, ...)
	for o, d in ipairs(FLASH_NAMES) do
		if string[u1[U1(W1(742834 + -802574), -993524 + 27460468136679)]](string[u1[U1(W1(562790 - 625108), 2454285757305 - (-973053))]](K[u1[U1(W1(96969 - 156461), 565880 + 1283010125789)]]), string[u1[U1(W1(798592 + -858089), 207434 + 34839268971797)]](d)) then
			return true;
		end;
	end;
	local o = K[u1[U1(W1(-945293 + 883023), 19870518491076 - (-142432))]] and K[u1[U1(W1(555365 + -617404), -138783 + 12721346917698)]][u1[U1(W1(-578004 + 515517), 11853142989973 - (-456954))]]:match(u1[U1(W1(-453376 - (-391216)), -45395 + 23006920088364)]) or u1[U1(W1(-948566 - (-888852)), 24710452183761 - (-125473))];
	for K, d in ipairs(FLASH_IDS) do
		if o == d then
			return true;
		end;
	end;
	return false;
end;

local function a(K, ...)
	if not K then
		return u1[U1(W1(-735226 - (-673540)), 1034942 + 28728072792451)];
	end;
	local o = K:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(-59508 + -1741), 1031256 + 15178990411588)]);
	if o and o:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(17803 + -79217), 475262 + 1206138077619)]) then
		return tostring(o[u1[U1(W1(538764 + -601093), -427716 + 7414371521600)]][u1[U1(W1(946470 + -1008388), 1442461853273 - (-760902))]]);
	end;
	if K:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(-878456 + 817619), -678072 + 27879982467950)]) and K[u1[U1(W1(689855 + -749464), 24460427577459 - (-582667))]]:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(437364 - 498501), 656234 + 22933046814285)]) then

	end;
	return u1[U1(W1(3910 - 63341), 29667620631611 - (-901564))];
end;

function ClearTracers(...)
	for K, o in pairs(TracersList) do
		pcall(function(...)
			if o[u1[U1(W1(163928 - 223410), 326155 + 5283090239377)]] then
				o:Remove();
			elseif o[u1[U1(W1(-400080 + 339216), 7899214235527 - (-902871))]] then
				o:Destroy();
			end;
		end);
	end;
	TracersList = {};
end;
function UpdateTracers(...)
	if not ESPMaster or not ESPTracers then
		ClearTracers();
		return;
	end;
	local K = Vector2[u1[U1(W1(187649 + -247405), 757358 + 8865895563698)]](camera[u1[U1(W1(-517141 + 456933), -421573 + 12186693010671)]][u1[U1(W1(861686 + -923883), 28593888951148 - 853865)]] / (197123 + -197121), camera[u1[U1(W1(-89035 - (-27626)), 272082 + 10078209633155)]][u1[U1(W1(-934242 + 872859), 15994678313963 - (-399661))]] - 1016932618 % 7212288);
	local o = {};
	for K, d in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if d ~= player and (d[u1[U1(W1(-1028028 - (-965149)), -901367 + 29006391915962)]] and d[u1[U1(W1(466829 - 527350), 15598153884042 - 1017131)]]:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(222842 - 282752), -62836 + 27352329807668)])) then
			local K = d[u1[U1(W1(305089 + -367079), -702208 + 25086171423905)]]:FindFirstChildOfClass(u1[U1(W1(-418399 + 356659), 871475 + 28490165544863)]);
			if K and K[u1[U1(W1(783694 - 845930), 758074 + 9469228974079)]] > 379192200 % 15799675 then
				local K, V = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(d[u1[U1(W1(492157 + -553283), 1751965606598 - (-467049))]][u1[U1(W1(423881 - 486376), 876238 + 19865064310100)]][u1[U1(W1(1037149 + -1099855), 520903 + 25407368297328)]]);
				if V then
					table[u1[U1(W1(-651488 - (-591332)), 8774509916142 - 385017)]](o, { [u1[U1(W1(591019 - 652632), 4246824636723 - 461178)]] = d, [u1[U1(W1(79356 + -139769), 659466 + 18230662172959)]] = Vector2[u1[U1(W1(-683678 - (-623711)), 331757 + 21920323033074)]](K[u1[U1(W1(746495 - 806650), 5474830612660 - 569023)]], K[u1[U1(W1(-265461 - (-204529)), -359844 + 4621100582411)]]) });
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	if Drawing then
		for o, d in ipairs(o) do
			local V = TracersList[o];
			if not V then
				V = Drawing[u1[U1(W1(-859930 + 800549), -926005 + 30112008001530)]](u1[U1(W1(930794 + -992541), 749086818861 - 884945)]);
				V[u1[U1(W1(869634 - 932316), 169307 + 26566282828535)]] = 1333627069 % 14655242.5;
				V[u1[U1(W1(298658 + -361065), 25853135635492 - (-1010402))]] = .8;
				TracersList[o] = V;
			end;
			V[u1[U1(W1(73956 + -135045), 25709468076646 - 514979)]] = Color3[u1[U1(W1(545419 + -606053), -801946 + 33375643711823)]](1270470029 % 6167329, 675443 + -675188, 1140909341 % 5973346);
			V[u1[U1(W1(-981051 - (-919382)), 11934192114280 - 1033395)]] = K;
			V[u1[U1(W1(-533254 - (-473446)), -168033 + 4562374809773)]] = d[u1[U1(W1(-824239 + 763032), -702789 + 31548470588071)]];
			V[u1[U1(W1(953929 - 1013891), 17217282423973 - 777556)]] = true;
		end;
		for K = #o + 2891066031 % 14527970, #TracersList, -758427 - (-758428) do
			if TracersList[K] then
				TracersList[K][u1[U1(W1(-367461 - (-307378)), 351206 + 12461589428932)]] = false;
			end;
		end;
	end;
end;
function ClearESP(...)
	for K, o in pairs(ESPObjects) do
		if o and o[u1[U1(W1(-689536 + 629674), -881469 + 5229994982592)]] then
			o:Destroy();
		end;
	end;
	ESPObjects = {};
end;
function UpdateESP(...)
	ClearESP();
	if not ESPMaster then
		return;
	end;
	for K, o in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if o ~= player and o[u1[U1(W1(-207934 + 146694), -263058 + 29403974498574)]] then
			CreateESP(o[u1[U1(W1(637086 - 698764), 839699 + 5871104932506)]]);
		end;
	end;
end;
Players[u1[U1(W1(-875911 + 815221), 15308204044101 - 21357)]]:Connect(function(K, ...)
	K[u1[U1(W1(-379833 - (-318795)), 10214281606554 - 746246)]]:Connect(function(K, ...)
		task[u1[U1(W1(-964387 + 903809), 846821 + 34877495956364)]](265027 + -265026.5);
		if ESPMaster then
			CreateESP(K);
		end;
	end);
end);
for K, o in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
	if o ~= player then
		o[u1[U1(W1(-878994 - (-819602)), 30906356581526 - (-308129))]]:Connect(function(K, ...)
			task[u1[U1(W1(-750371 + 690504), 2382238432740 - (-67185))]](312165.5 - 312165);
			if ESPMaster then
				CreateESP(K);
			end;
		end);
	end;
end;
function UpdateESPData(...)
	if not ESPMaster then
		ClearTracers();
		return;
	end;
	local K = player[u1[U1(W1(-422899 + 361058), 90123 + 24779295329543)]];
	local o = K and K:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(196342 - 256288), 672886 + 24212150527892)]);
	if not o then
		return;
	end;
	for K, d in pairs(ESPObjects) do
		if d and d[u1[U1(W1(-241114 + 180530), 11851467316490 - (-915588))]] then
			local K = d[u1[U1(W1(155242 + -217821), -445184 + 26531261938498)]][u1[U1(W1(942378 - 1005374), 8892843666457 - (-615444))]];
			if K then
				local V = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(K);
				local M = K:FindFirstChildOfClass(u1[U1(W1(240327 + -300332), -829979 + 2074465791456)]);
				local X = K:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(-852194 - (-792229)), 121398882075 - (-53533))]);
				if M and X then
					local K = M[u1[U1(W1(-645368 - (-584487)), 264095 + 13139668209735)]] / M[u1[U1(W1(-24517 + -38073), -158853 + 20305127952756)]];
					local l = d:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(-25687 + -36544), 70207 + 22041722230232)]);
					if l then

		end);

function getClosestPlayer(K, ...)
	local o = player[u1[U1(W1(372673 + -434659), -626188 + 4563713234521)]] and player[u1[U1(W1(-504861 + 444770), 12392666684075 - (-818143))]]:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(-263780 + 202715), 19811489524097 - (-326280))]);
	if not o then
		return nil;
	end;
	local d = K or maxRange;
	if AimlockTargetPlayer ~= u1[U1(W1(524720 - 586784), 637703 + 8260284748799)] and AimlockTargetPlayer ~= nil then
		local K = Players:FindFirstChild(AimlockTargetPlayer);
		if K and (K[u1[U1(W1(-833294 + 773272), 23390746267897 - (-252307))]] and K[u1[U1(W1(-758795 - (-696404)), 797680 + 6488444772317)]]:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(-694553 + 634261), -436025 + 28050360212030)])) then
			local o = K[u1[U1(W1(785824 - 845634), 30213651029580 - 894807)]]:FindFirstChildOfClass(u1[U1(W1(595793 - 656793), -480189 + 25949151920880)]);
			if o and o[u1[U1(W1(-464500 - (-402357)), 258677 + 8873395330478)]] > -505763 + 505763 then
				return K[u1[U1(W1(-91999 + 32647), 836886 + 7891286231364)]];
			end;
		end;
	end;
	local V, M = nil, math[u1[U1(W1(-606772 + 544997), 211759 + 7517987705469)]];
	for K, X in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if X ~= player and (not BlacklistedPlayers[X[u1[U1(W1(-459370 - (-398061)), 952528 + 16820120868296)]]] and (X[u1[U1(W1(-940341 + 877475), 29174640618990 - 555229)]] and X[u1[U1(W1(-127714 + 68263), 473901 + 11158622083359)]]:FindFirstChild(u1[U1(W1(82539 - 143202), 5733946199002 - 704876)]))) then
			local K = X[u1[U1(W1(-623596 + 562170), 362061951645 - 930293)]]:FindFirstChildOfClass(u1[U1(W1(883353 - 943655), 28217944998625 - 249741)]);
			if K and K[u1[U1(W1(827198 + -887181), 19692082772220 - (-207573))]] > 629258760 % 12101130 then
				local K = ((X[u1[U1(W1(-930093 - (-870372)), 29081131884912 - 666763)]][u1[U1(W1(-454839 - (-393297)), 752230 + 23632980570262)]][u1[U1(W1(304341 + -367167), 1465457803209 - (-738017))]] - o[u1[U1(W1(671207 - 734069), 30083862101112 - (-47680))]]))[u1[U1(W1(-1047435 + 986567), 19445453551442 - (-1043468))]];
				if K < M and K < d then
					M = K;
					V = X[u1[U1(W1(267807 - 329069), 4852942820779 - (-13096))]];
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	return V;
end;

function ClearAura(...)
	for K, o in pairs(AuraObjects) do
		if o and o[u1[U1(W1(744660 - 806541), 307906 + 29747955876698)]] then
			o:Destroy();
		end;
	end;
	AuraObjects = {};
end;
function ApplyAura(K, ...)
	ClearAura();
	if not K then
		ActiveAura = nil;
		return;
	end;
	local o = AURA_DEFS[K];
	if not o then
		ActiveAura = nil;
